## Identity and objective

The third defect is plan-self-test throughput, not a missing semantic check. The 39-event development restart above completed rows 1–58 before the plan child exhausted its unchanged outer bound. The repaired self-test leaves every case, output contract, claim, receipt transition, verifier row, and deadline in place. ### Sealed C4 CLI-profile execution boundary — historical capability snapshot

The sealed and immutable C4 boundary is anchored at commit `c4089ab31181c08dad880bf9e5d40c622c8c91c4`, tree `f427e23b6fe297a49a36a99fa037073b637d82ca`. The late C4 red-team changed the implementation rather than rubber-stamping it. Legal dual-16-MiB capture could otherwise exceed the private store after duplicated encoding, and post-child `ReadDir`/recursive removal could let candidate-created trees drive unbounded work. The repaired design pre-admits a 48 MiB unique-body envelope before owner acquisition, gives Drain/Captured references one shared persisted raw frame, caps actual drafts at 15 logical kinds/14 unique blobs before persistence, bounds direct rosters at exact cardinality plus one, and removes only retained named leaves and proven-empty roots. Hostile deep store/evidence/scope residue is refused or marked ambiguous and is deliberately preserved. Focused didrun passes and read-only critics support development only; the exact 80-event final ledger remains the sole C4 receipt source.

The first fresh C4 final ledger is preserved under `.didrun-history/p07b-c-c4-final-failed-3acc549d211f-attempt1/.didrun`: commands 1–22 passed, but command 23—the exact standalone-closure 20-run qualification—reached the unchanged 1,200-second limit. Those 22 events are nonreusable. The repaired development topology passed its selected two-run closure (`95.051s`), complete CLI-package two-run (`283.550s`), and exact 20-run qualification (`1022.843s`); an additive race-instrumented diagnostic ended after `272.937s` when instrumentation exceeded the current terminal-closure deadline, emitted no data-race report, and earns no race grade. All 80 final grades remain `UNRECEIPTED`, and the next final ledger restarts at command 1.

The second fresh C4 final ledger and private root are preserved at `.didrun-history/p07b-c-c4-final-failed-aca5f714fc9a-attempt2/.didrun/` and `.countershape/p07bc-c4-final-failed-aca5f714fc9a-attempt2/`. Runbook commands 1–77 passed and were immediately claimed on staged tree `aca5f714fc9aa6b29afffc58fe21c2a9bdeb883f`; command 78 then failed before scope evaluation because its first `/usr/bin/git` inventory child exited zero with nonempty stderr. The checker correctly failed closed but retained only the child status/signal/error tuple, so this event cannot prove the discarded bytes. The symptom matches the already-recorded managed-sandbox Apple `confstr()` warning class, but that is an inference rather than this event's established cause. All 77 green events are permanent nonreusable diagnostics. C4 cannot repair the parent-owned scope checker because that path is outside its frozen 38-path-plus-three-prefix authority. That inspection is executable rather than export-decorative. The checker has no top-level repetition-helper import. Through C4V it first requires exact legacy helper SHA-256 `1321fb1381f1f74d0959b9ceb40b4dcc4abc637bb281ff2af759863ede3bfd5a`; at C4/C5 it reads pinned, stage-equal mode-`100644` helper/runtime/verifier bytes and runs a hermetic Acorn static closure check. At live C4/C5, `qualificationStaticAuthority` decodes the recursively frozen literal catalog into a checker-owned projection and never imports or evaluates future candidate helper bytes. The static gate does not execute candidate parser, selector, validator, or builder exports; actual execution evidence is the separate helper self-test, per-case receipts, and preseal reconciliation. The helper's sole relative named-import edge is `./verify-runtime-authority.mjs`; the runtime has Node-built-in imports only; `verify-current.mjs` has the same sole relative edge. Re-exports, dynamic import, CommonJS or dynamic-evaluation loaders including `process.getBuiltinModule`, side-effect or aliased imports, effectful module initialization, authority-wrapper forwarding, and noncanonical direct entry fail. The checker validates the projected recursively frozen C4/C5 records, exact digests, C4 legacy aliases, and `qualificationCaseForID(caseID)` stable identity for all 56 IDs while rejecting unknown IDs. This is source/import/catalog conformance, not a JavaScript sandbox or proof of unexecuted C5 packages.

At preseal reconciliation, the checker independently derives the exact ordered `[go,node,git,sh,cc,cxx]` roster from frozen `COUNTERSHAPE_GO`, `COUNTERSHAPE_NODE`, `COUNTERSHAPE_GIT`, `COUNTERSHAPE_SH`, `COUNTERSHAPE_CC`, and `COUNTERSHAPE_CXX`; it realpaths each authority, requires an executable regular non-symlink file, performs a bounded no-follow raw-byte read of at most 512 MiB with before/after identity, SHA-256 fingerprints it, and requires the Node realpath to equal the invoking checker. Every one of the 54/56 event stdout blobs must match that independently admitted roster exactly; the terminal authority digest is SHA-256 of UTF-8 `JSON.stringify(roster)`, every event shares it, and the checker reopens the roster after all blobs. A self-consistent foreign stdout roster is rejected. This is bounded preseal-time independent admission plus post-blob re-admission and recorded per-event output, not proof that executable bytes stayed continuously unchanged between event execution and reconciliation, across events, or against same-uid swap-and-restore/TOCTOU.

C4 must preserve the ordinary `--case <id>` execution path and export `qualificationCaseForID(caseID)` as the one stable selector. The helper's recursively frozen `qualificationMatrices` remain the literal C4/C5 catalog authority. The exported qualification path is parse → canonicalize → validate → build → run. `parseRunArguments(argv)` accepts the exact `--case <id>` form and directly returns `qualificationCaseForID(argv[1])`; its next fail-closed preamble rejects malformed selector shapes before ad-hoc parsing. For a named qualification, `validateExecutionSpecification(specification)` reselects `specification.caseID` through that same selector, rejects any copied, drifted, or non-identical specification, and returns the canonical frozen object. `buildGoTestArguments(specification)` derives the exact frozen Go argv from that validated object. `runRepetition(specification, dependencies = {})` begins with `const executionSpecification = validateExecutionSpecification(specification)` and may build or execute only from `executionSpecification`, never the unvalidated input. `main()` ends with the exact awaited parse-to-run route `await runRepetition(parseRunArguments(process.argv.slice(2)))` before the final exact `pathToFileURL`-bound direct guard awaits `main()`. That direct-guard sentence records the immutable C4 helper source form; sealed C4H established it, and sealed C4I, C4K, C4J, and C4L preserved it while the cumulative verifier uses its separately checked canonical-entry classifier and noncanonical spelling refusal.

During C4, the helper self-test must drive both dormant C5-only identities—`contract-http-readiness-50` and `contract-http-teardown-20`—through the actual exported parser, selector, validator, and argument builder, assert stable canonical identity and the exact frozen Go argv, and exercise the dependency-injected run entry without acquiring runtime authority or spawning an absent C5 package. Unknown IDs, wrong flags, extra arguments, copied specifications, raw C4-alias lookup, and builder drift must fail before child execution. C4's 54 real case commands prove the live catalog, and C5's 56 real case commands later prove the parent-sealed superset. Every command independently acquires and releases runtime authority, so failure in one receipt cannot suppress, inherit, or lend evidence to another. The static closure and synthetic catalog hostiles protect the declaration; real per-case runs, helper/runtime self-tests, and preseal stdout-blob reconciliation protect execution, child-result/JSON validation, cleanup, and finalization.

`--print-final-runbook C4V`, `--print-final-runbook C4`, `--print-final-runbook C4H`, `--print-final-runbook C4I`, `--print-final-runbook C4K`, `--print-final-runbook C4J`, `--print-final-runbook C4L`, and `--print-final-runbook C5` render these exact historical contracts. `C3V-S6-DIDRUN-INTERRUPT-1`: during an unclaimed development run, operator SIGINT caused didrun to exit `130` with a Python `KeyboardInterrupt` traceback before it appended an event for the in-flight cumulative verifier; process inspection found no surviving verifier child. It left the mode-`0600` lock for PID `84735` in place; review proved the PID absent, no process held the file, and the owner, repository-root digest, and mode matched before one explicit didrun-wrapped recovery event removed only that lock. The interrupted command therefore has no didrun receipt and supports no claim. This is a didrun interruption-capture bug finding, not a verifier failure or a sealed-tree result; final evidence must complete normally in a fresh ledger. That obligation is historical; sealed C3V later fulfilled it.

`C3-S6-DIDRUN-INTERRUPT-2`: during the active C3 build loop, a critic identified stale cumulative success markers while the development verifier was still running, so the obsolete child was interrupted. Process inspection found no surviving verifier, but didrun emitted a Python `KeyboardInterrupt` traceback and appended no ledger event. The interruption is `UNRECEIPTED`, supports no claim, and could not enter the final ledger. At that boundary C3 was required to replace it with a normal completion in the fresh eighteen-event final session; sealed C3 later did so.

### Active C3D data-driven receipt-phase machinery

C3D is the active exact seven-path `SOURCE_FULL` `DEFECT_REPAIR` at `sha256:ac4436680c612ad206d2786ddd3146af747e1f8ea2bdedf898afca67d46fcd63`, directly after sealed C3B and before C4. Immediate-parent order, exact key/state vocabulary, monotonic `ABSENT` to `PRESENT` transitions, zero receipt flips for `SOURCE_FULL`, and exactly one flip for `RECEIPT_RECONCILIATION` are structural invariants.

The visible current-state capsule above is rendered and classified only from that table. `renderReceiptPhaseCapsule`, `classifyReceiptPhase`, `rewriteReceiptPhaseCapsule`, and the generic fixture generator cover every declared row without adding a receipt-state branch for C4, C5, or C6A. The complete forward authority—boundary, parent, profile, exact paths, prefixes, receipt states, and receipt-claim manifests—is pinned at `sha256:91ace265208883f3df976cc6a58d4a9d816d9c7c9fd2ba58887ccab13c3561fc`. C3D is the only forward unit that owns `spec/verification/p07b-c-unit-paths.json`; C4, C5, C6A, C6M, and C6B cannot rewrite the table that selects their corpus. `--check-candidate-phase <unit>` builds a strict full-index source snapshot: it inventories the entire pinned index tree, requires equality with the live stage-zero index, loads every tracked blob by OID under bounded byte ceilings, and compares every tracked worktree file through no-follow stable reads before giving `checkPlan()` strict object-backed overrides. It also pins the exact parent commit/tree and didrun notes tree, matches the explicit unit argument, admits exactly one declared parent edge, mechanically invokes the separately implemented immutable unit-scope transition oracle, then rechecks HEAD, index, notes, and worktree authority. C6M owns neither checker. For C4 and later, candidate specification bytes must equal the C3D-sealed parent authority. C3D alone may bootstrap from the exact sealed C3B commit/tree with no prior capsule and may introduce v16; same-row replay, skip, downgrade, stale bytes, OID mismatch, and persistent authority or specification drift are refused. The transition catalog pins six admitted forward edges and 37 hostile snapshot cases, while the independent scope oracle pins six admitted edges and 20 separate generic hostiles plus future-C6 material cases. This is not same-user isolation: an actor that changes and restores refs or files between observations can create an ABA mutation, so the gate relies on the documented sole-writer discipline. The no-argument cumulative plan check remains a live-coherence check and is not transition evidence. The deterministic generic catalog contains 666 accepted/hostile/observed-state/rewrite cases at digest `479f21a8a86a8a6eb34056dabb471e8ceebf08c725008d5f204fa2294af9f83a`; four named executable fixed regressions extend it to 670 cases at combined digest `a56fc2d71b79a06ba151ec454e7ed9280b20140e41c7006e027091eeddcf13e7`. ## Why the original idea was killed

The first concept was a local, vendor-neutral agent execution runtime with a durable event journal, resume/replay/fork, evidence, and a causal viewer. Direct inspection showed [`nelsonwerd/wake`](https://github.com/nelsonwerd/wake) already implements that exact semantic spine deeply. Do not drift back.

The assurance-graph alternative was also parked because the installed didrun already owns command recording, Git-tree binding, claim grades, commit manifests, bundles, strict verification, and HTML evidence. Countershape may reference didrun grades verbatim; it must not reproduce or strengthen them.

The behavior-to-code tracing alternative was parked because Repro, Highlight, OpenReplay, Traceway, and related work already occupy much of browser/session replay, cross-layer traces, source mapping, and agent-facing context.

## Red-team-locked Choicepoint kernel

A Choicepoint contains:

- exact candidate Git tree ids and a regular/executable-blob-only materialization manifest;
- deterministic `WorldPlan`; opaque `CandidateExecutionBinding`; structural `WorldInstance`; later `InstanceMeasurements` and process receipts; and a declared `ComparisonEnvelope` that never claims behavioral compatibility;
- persisted per-repetition `ComparisonAdmission` matrices with a stimulus-independent comparison basis, or a pre-batch `RejectedComparison` that creates no token, trial, batch, or map;
- original and locally minimized replayable stimulus;
- `OBSERVED_STABLE(k/k)` evidence plus physically new confirmation attempts; no execution-evidence reuse;
- post-capture-policy CapturedObservation digests, tagged trials, peer candidate batches with the same ordered admission-digest set, and exact projected `CandidateOutcomeMap` with globally unique attempt/world evidence identities;
- `UNSTABLE`, `UNCOMPARABLE`, and `INCOMPLETE` members kept explicit and out of outcome cards;
- comparability-first tri-valued reduction: U1 owns only `PRESERVES`, `CHANGES`, `UNRESOLVED`, and logical sweep evidence; sealed U5 may award only `UNCHANGED`, `BEST_KNOWN`, or `ONE_MINIMAL_UNDER` under its durable authority;
- blind-first local-caller ruling: allow one/many, custom expectation, reject all, or defer may promote to the fixed `RULING` head; semantic `REFINE` is noncompilable and durable promotion refuses because successor-study construction is not implemented;
- exact-witness scope only, no-default selected fields, nonasserted fields, and mandatory allowed/disallowed separation;
- separate DecisionRecord, deterministic ContractBundle, pre-spawn ContractExecutionTarget, exact FinalizedContractRun, and fresh ContractExecution classification; derived wire facts such as `compilable` are reconstructed and never accepted as emitter authority;
- Go/Node semantic parity vectors and observer-produced closure across the five scoped standalone domains;
- receipt references without grade inflation.

The system does **not** establish global equivalence, completeness, correctness, security, maintainability, or production readiness.

## Hard non-duplication landmines

Stop if implementation becomes:

- agent launching, scheduling, crash recovery, run replay/fork, an event ledger, or an agent timeline (Wake);
- command receipt capture, Git-tree freshness grading, claim sealing, or another evidence bundle (didrun);
- a worktree/task board, AI arena, diff selector, winner ranker, merge/splice tool, or semantic merge-safety score (Parallel Code, Forge, Switchman, current research);
- baseline screenshot approval or generic visual regression;
- spec/plan/task generation or contract-first competitive implementation (Pact Tools, Spec Kit, VibeContract);
- a claim to have invented differential testing, behavioral clustering, ambiguity discovery, shrinking, approval testing, or executable contracts.

## Completed deep-dive ruling

All six specialist lanes, synthesis, and adversarial red team are complete under `research/deep-dive/`. The red team granted only a conditional go for a narrowed Darwin reference instrument. It cut symlinks, general compatible-world language, execution-evidence reuse, broad imported-repository/cross-platform claims, and safe/shareable-report language. It made Go/Node parity and a falsification-first stateful proof blockers.

## P07 implementation-time red-team correction

The preimplementation P07 review found that sealed U6 could not support its original prompt literally:

- every then-fresh Choicepoint constructed only the whole projection, so its DecisionRecord could not authorize `http.status`, `cli.stdout.json.mode`, an unselected-field change, or a selected-only custom `401` expectation;
- canonical stimuli retain content digests but not every stdin/body/fixture/seed byte needed to run a standalone test;
- rewriting adapter projection envelopes would invalidate sealed fingerprints and downstream evidence;
- the inherited-listener HTTP start cannot be reproduced by a reference-quality Node-only parent, and Node's high-level HTTP client normalizes bytes differently from the Go wire parser;
- the old ContractBundle/ContractExecution planning schemas placed a self digest inside its own preimage, omitted recoverable file bytes, and mixed later absence evidence into deterministic source; and
- the first corrected ContractExecution plan still tried to reuse a 2–4-candidate historical `WorldInstance` for an arbitrary later tree and admitted an unproven generic repository executable; and
- terminal `RESIDUE` cannot also act as a mutable execution head.

The locked correction in `research/deep-dive/11-p07-implementation-red-team.md` is:

1. **P07A/U6b — implemented and sealed:** preserve every historical CLI/HTTP projection byte and the 27-member Choicepoint body; strictly translate roster-verified adapter wires under the exact projection binding into a closed portable algebra; make fresh Choicepoints use `ADAPTER_BOUND_PORTABLE_FIELDS_V1`; add real selectable/differing fields and selected-only custom expectations; keep legacy whole-projection records byte-identical and nonemittable.
2. **P07B/U6c:** require the current portable ruling plus a byte-complete `PortableSource` that reconstructs the exact plan/binding/minimized stimulus and matches every verified confirmation execution-binding digest; independently retranslate reopened confirmation projections rather than trusting source-carried tuples; require a new physical HTTP plan → observation → confirmation → portable Choicepoint → selected-field ruling lineage under `NODE_LOOPBACK_CHILD_BIND_PIPE_READY_V1`, leaving inherited-listener P07A rulings structurally nonemittable; support only the antecedent-backed logical-Node-plus-repository-script launch; compile a recoverable six-file bundle; stale-check before publication; make post-residue materialization retryable; use raw `node:net` parity; let only C3 publish/reopen a capability-only `ContractExecutionTarget` for one explicit pinned tree whose objects are directly checked, fresh attempt, measured boot session, and admitted runtime; let only the matching sealed CLI runner or active HTTP runner acquire the private boot-session interlock, record target-keyed intent, and consume the winner-only process-local permit while low-level mechanics sees no semantic authority; keep missing SpawnObservation ineligible and the interlock held; publish a matching `FinalizedContractRun` only after a bounded owner-produced witness closes separate primary-plus-cleanup process control and `COMPLETE | PARTIAL | VIOLATED` standalone axes; and publish `ContractExecution` as immutable classifier-profile-bound derived classification with no independently supplied tuple or process/scope reason and no contract-subject process start or rerun on classification retry. Live target recovery may still execute bounded owned runtime probe processes.

This is a prerequisite repair, not a scope reduction. P07A selected-field authority is explicitly not yet export-ready source authority.

## Smallest proof now locked

Two local dependency-free Node-core domains, no model credential:

- **HTTP authorization:** four candidates: stable `403`, stable `404`, stable metadata-bearing `200`, and a deliberate alternating candidate. Volatile fields and shared-root contamination are physical negative controls. A same-request/same-roster physical seed neighbor changes `403|404|200` to `200|500|500`; a separate synthetic same-partition reducer vector changes `403|404|404` to `200|500|500`. The system must expose or reject each under its exact authority.
- **CLI precedence:** three green-test candidates receive the same nonempty config, environment, and argv values but disagree on which source wins.

The proof must show that fresh roots, repetitions, visible projection, the exact labeled map, physical fresh confirmation, selected-field separation, and absent-runtime execution each prevent a concrete false result. `CUSTOM_EXPECTATION`, not reject-all, is the only `none conforms` path.

## didrun discipline — exact operating rule

`.didrun/` is secret-bearing, self-ignored by didrun, excluded from tree digests, and must never be committed. A redundant root `.didrun/` ignore rule is intentionally absent because it triggers the measured didrun 0.1.0 tree-digest bug recorded in `docs/status/DIDRUN_BUGS.md`.

For every shippable build unit:

1. run every load-bearing test/lint/build/integration command through `didrun run -- <command>`;
2. immediately after each final successful command, declare only its honest claim before recording another event; use `didrun show --session` for diagnosis and review, never as an invitation to delay or explicitly rebind claims;
3. stage intentional files and commit without an AI `Co-Authored-By` trailer;
4. in this managed environment, run `didrun seal --commit HEAD` only with Git-note write authority, then require `git notes --ref=didrun show HEAD` to succeed;
5. run `NO_COLOR=1 didrun verify --strict`;
6. on nonzero: the unit is not done—fix the cause, rerun through didrun, re-claim, commit, reseal, and reverify; never weaken/delete/relabel a failed claim.

All six steps are single-writer operations. Never launch two `didrun run`, claim, seal, or verify commands concurrently, including from one orchestration call. Bounded read-only critics may run in parallel; build commands, repository writes, Git, and didrun remain serialized.

The final commit also needs a didrun HTML report and a receipt table mapping every claimed capability to the **verbatim grade**. Anything not passed through didrun is `UNRECEIPTED`.

Its behavioral cross-study matrix covers one valid same-plan/projection/profile source with different stimulus/execution binding and one valid same-adapter/runner/projection/profile/stimulus/capture source with a different WorldPlan/execution binding. Same-adapter runner drift is unconstructible under the closed source API; profile, entrypoint/start, HTTP readiness/capture, proof, selected-field, and partition drift remain upstream-plus-architecture obligations rather than independently behaviorally receipted branches. It does not establish continued currentness, private input-body exposure, compiler output, bundle recovery, publication, materialization, execution, secret absence, containment, portability, production readiness, security review, or adoption. The full limits, failed-receipt history, and sole next boundary are in `docs/status/P07B-A2-1-AUTHORITY.md`.

The sealed predecessor architecture chain is `P07B-B -> P07B-A2.2 -> P07B-A1 -> U6/P07A-B -> U5`; the evolved A2.2 checker retains A2.1 closure internally rather than exposing a duplicate top-level row that points at the same file. The current B gate also invokes the complete C1 inert-model checker through a separate one-way `B -> C1` compatibility edge; C1 never invokes B. U1–U4 architecture gates and every real frozen mutation gate are explicit historical sealed-unit contracts: the one-command baseline prints them as `HISTORICAL-ONLY NOT-RUN`, does not spawn them, and never calls them passed on today's tree. The proposed A2.2 source-rewrite driver never became a checked-in executable and remains `UNRECEIPTED`, so it appears in neither executable roster. The B defensive self-test uses cloned metadata/topology negatives rather than rewritten production sources and makes no mutation-completeness claim. ### Pre-A2 planning-wire correction

Before any runtime bundle codec or compiler existed, the follow-up prompt-pack
red team found that the planning-only whole-bundle `contains_*` flags were
unsound. Authorized exact source, predicate, digest-linked, and rendered human
content may legitimately contain sensitive, host-looking, candidate-looking,
receipt-looking, or order-bearing bytes. The planning schema/example/generator/
validator now freeze scope
`EMITTER_INVENTED_STRUCTURAL_FACTS_EXCLUDING_AUTHORIZED_INPUT_CONTENT`, seven
structural `emitter_introduces_*` false flags, and
`confidentiality_established = false`. These are emitter-structure nonclaims,
not content scanning or redaction. The same preimplementation correction makes
`CUSTOM_EXPECTATION` cardinality exactly one, caps the declared repository-
relative entrypoint at 4,096 ASCII bytes, rejects a root-leading hyphen to match
the sealed shared runner grammar, and locks the exact outer/predicate/file/
dependency/confidentiality constants in the validator. It changes no sealed A1 byte, digest,
fixture, receipt, or claim and creates no compiler capability. Preserve the
corrected exact scope; a different runtime wire requires a new version and a
separate reviewed boundary.

### U6 operational facts that P07A/P07B must preserve

- **Store/layout:** closed `countershape/v1` canonical payloads live under `objects/sha256/<prefix>/<digest>`; mutable heads live under `studies/<derived-study-id>/head.json`; private capture material has a separate private directory. This is a local Darwin reference layout, not a stable public migration format.
- **Head spine:** `SOURCE_PLAN -> BASELINE -> DIVERGENCE -> REDUCTION -> CONFIRMATION -> CHOICEPOINT_READY -> RULING -> RESIDUE`. U6a and P07A stop at `RULING`; sealed P07B-B owns the sole construction-safe terminal ContractBundle `RESIDUE` edge. No unit adds branching lineage, archived history, partial resume, or cancelled resume. ContractExecutionTarget, FinalizedContractRun, and ContractExecution remain separate immutable nonhead objects.
- **Freshness:** confirmation creates a fresh 32-byte challenge, derives an exact ordinal nonce, requires finalized `CONFIRMATION` process-edge facts, and enforces current-run plus discovery/reduction evidence disjointness. This is relative physical-fixture evidence, not hostile-process attestation or timestamp truth.
- **Blind kernel:** the closed DTO omits system-supplied candidate/ref/producer identity, support counts, total candidate count, source order, and reveal data; fingerprint-only aliases/order ignore support. Caller-authored scenario or behavior content may still reveal identity, and U6 says nothing about DOM/accessibility/CSS behavior.
- **Attribution:** a DecisionRecord says `LOCAL_CALLER_ASSERTED_OPERATOR` and `AUTHENTICITY_NOT_ESTABLISHED_IN_U6`. Review-surface markers prove recorded presentation only, not comprehension or debiasing.
- **Receipt behavior:** didrun grades are inert verbatim strings; missing remains `UNRECEIPTED`; Countershape never interprets receipt text as conformance authority.
- **Resource ceilings:** canonical input/object 1 MiB; study label and actor 512 bytes; Choicepoint scenario 8 KiB; nested Choicepoint evidence 600 KiB; reveal display/provenance 2 KiB; decision annotation 32 KiB; confirmation projection proof 512 KiB. These are parser/orchestration bounds, not hostile same-user resource isolation.
